### 2e.5 Signing a lane's DRAFTED shift record at its merge point (point 38's form), and a point's own gate

1. Merge the lane (`git merge --no-ff --no-commit origin/<lane>`), resolve, commit the merge (its hash names the record).
2. Fill the draft's `seat` and replace its `[MERGE SEAT: …]` marker in `cause` with the merge commit; write it to `docs/shift-records/<date>-style-2-merge-<n>-<slug>.json` (house name form). Keep the lane's DRAFT file in the tree until the door has run (the door refuses a dirty tree: deleting the draft first gave "REFUSED [DIRTY_TREE]"); the new record may be untracked.
3. `GOLDEN_SHIFT_SIGNED=docs/shift-records/<file> UPDATE_GOLDEN=1 npx vitest run <the surface's suite>` — fails BY DESIGN with "golden RE-RECORDED through the signed door … sha256 A -> B; rows m -> n"; it must read the lane's predicted figures exactly, else STOP.
4. Restate by hand what the door's write moves (point 38: the herald suite's `toHaveLength(442)` → 446 with its dated comment); `git rm` the draft; `python3 tree.py <n> <record> <LANE>` (the record joins the roster's `tree` class, else sourceCitationIntegrity reds "the archival roster moved"); plain run of the suite + `tests/lint/goldenFreeze.walker.test.js` + `tests/lint/sourceCitationIntegrity.walker.test.js`; commit with `Owner-Signed: §934.105` and the seat trailer (44d4f68e1 is the form).
- **A point's gate** (Appendix F `gate.sh <n> <govfile> [vd]`, background, timeout 7200000): build, `node scripts/boot-smoke.mjs`, strict (1113), `typecheck:ratchet` (167), `npx vitest run --maxWorkers=2 tests/lint`, `tests/scripts`, the governing files (every test naming a changed src module by basename + the lane's own tests + `tests/property/generatorGoldenMaster.test.js` + `tests/lint/mutationCoverageManifest.test.js`), `npm run verify:dist`. Then the six surfaces: `sh scripts/audit/landing-stage.sh build <tip> /home/user/stages/sN && sh scripts/audit/landing-stage.sh measure /home/user/stages/sN /home/user/stages/mN /home/user/stages/m<prev>` — the m41 measurement died with the container: rebuild it by staging 2cbe72ca1 (0 on all six from m39 to m41). Run the lighting walker first; the census almost always moves (a new test file).

### 2e.6 What the next seat must not rediscover

- **The roster** (`tests/lint/.source-citation-baseline.json` + the walker header): a lane's `dated` +1 composes with the line's as a union, `dated` = ours + 1 (Appendix F `roster.py`; its note line needs a trailing comma when the conflict is mid-object — fixed). When a lane already merged the line's tip (FOUNDING-WORLDS did), there is no conflict and its own +1 stands. Every new shift record = `tree.py` (+1 `tree`). At hand-back: `dated` 237, `tree` 175.
- **The lighting census** (`tests/lint/.lighting-census-baseline.json`): take the LINE's side, then on a committed tree `LIGHTING_CENSUS_REFREEZE='<seat>' LIGHTING_CENSUS_NOTE='<cause>' npx vitest run tests/lint/sovereigntyLightingContract.walker.test.js` (it fails by design), commit, plain re-run green. At hand-back: files 3199, credited 2802, titles 29087, suite 7865.
- **The mutation-coverage manifest** (`scripts/mutation-coverage-manifest.json`): every NEW invariant test file a lane adds needs an entry, or `tests/lint/mutationCoverageManifest.test.js` TOTALITY reds. Lanes forget (GEN-SPEED ×2, CI-FIT ×1). Form: break the guarded code in place (copy first), run the pin, see the named arm red, restore from the copy, `cmp`, then a `kind: 'rationale'` entry quoting the red (8ad9aa39d at point 39, 7985fc779 at point 40).
- **The wiring census** moves with every new `src/` module (`node scripts/wiring-census.mjs` then `--check`; producerIndexFiles at hand-back 1319).
- **proseDrawnAnchors**: a lane's test literal that aligns ≥ 18 fixed chars against a state-prose variant reds as a NEW anchor; if it is the card's/data's own words (not drawn prose), carry it in `FROZEN_LITERAL_ANCHORS` with its reason (95f31f192, and point 41's foundingWorldBones:82).
- **The edge bundles**: never hand-merge; take either side, `npm run build:edge-shared`, and at the END keep all six metas from one run (the window test).
- **The ledger ref**: `git fetch origin review-fixes-2026-07-08:review-fixes-2026-07-08` before any tests/scripts or test:ratchet run.
- **Heap**: since point 40 `npm run build` carries 4096 itself; the dist sweep and stage builds call vite with the same flag.
- **Never** run a door on a dirty tree; never read a gate through a pipe (`sh scripts/gate-tail.sh …`); push with `git push --no-verify origin HEAD:lane/integration-2026-11-07` after `git fetch` + `git merge-base --is-ancestor origin/<line> HEAD`.

With the seed fixed the corpus builds. BUT the corpus builder is a DETECTOR SOURCE of the observed-shape instrument: a plain `--write` is REFUSED ("an ordinary gate/write cannot migrate the instrument"); it needs a governed migration to a target schema past PLAYED_WORLDS = 41 (the tooling's ceiling). **The chair's ruling (10:20 UTC): (B) MIGRATE NOW, on six conditions** — (1) ONE PACKAGE, its own commits, all green or none: the seed re-searched by the header's rule (state the rule and the three candidates, osr-tier-19 the lowest), the next target schema with its own named constant and cause ("the agreement lane's generation moved the tier seed's town out of the overlap"), the writer-reach re-key as measured, the re-freeze; (2) **THE TOOLING GAINS A ROW, NOT A BRANCH**: if re-governing needs more than a target constant, a row in the predecessor table and an entry for its validator, as the last migrations did, STOP — that is instrument design, not a merge act; take (A) and say so, and the chair seats an instrument seat; (3) **THE RECONCILIATION GOES TO THE CHAIR BEFORE `--migrate-schema` IS APPLIED**: how many identities move, each classed WITNESSED (the new corpus carries the key: one town and the writer named) or BINDING LOST (the read stands and no longer resolves: coverage lost), any NEW finding traced to its read and its missing writer — exposure named, a read or write the lane ADDED is a stop; the chair answers in one message; (4) writer reach accepted as measured, in point 46's form; (5) proseFamilyContract, once it loads: its figures by form (d); (6) say THIN before starting if the package would leave the seat unable to carry the rest. ### 2h.7 The forms ruled at point 46 (binding every point; as the chair gave them to this seat)
(a) A golden is signed at the merge point ONLY if it equals the lane's draft figure exactly; otherwise STOP. The field diff: every moved leaf lies in a family the draft declares. (At 47 the lane's base differs from the line's: law 2's second case — the successor puts the measured-vs-draft question to the chair if the draft's figures, taken on 3575cf061, cannot be equalled on the composed tree.)
(b) A register door runs in its no-op form (2f) when no cohort moves. When one moves: first a table BY KIND, with the town and person id folded out; a kind already banked is a carrier swap (a rising count included, with its reason); a kind not banked before is banked only if its write stands unchanged on the line (an old write newly witnessed); a write the lane added is NEVER banked at a merge: STOP. Rows of the reads-with-no-writer register that leave are named one by one as WITNESSED or BINDING LOST.
(c) The fork census: a FALL of a figure with its caller named is the seat's to key (both constants, one commit, before any bundle is emitted); a RISE goes back to the lane.
(d) A contract test whose figures come from the corpus joins the closing acts' class only if every moved figure is the movement the draft declares and no figure of the behaviour itself moves in kind.
(e) A worked-example town may be swapped only for the FIRST qualifying town in the test's own corpus order, every other assertion unchanged, k of n reported. No hunting; no assertion touched.
(f) The tuning register and the repetition guard move only through their own rituals and doors, shrink only, and only at a point where the chair has named them.
(g) Every red at a point is classed and named with its owner, against the line before the merge AND against the lane's own reviewed base. Never bank a baseline row, skip a test or loosen a pin.

## 5. The gate per point (the exact commands)

1. **Merge.** `git merge --no-ff --no-commit origin/<lane>`. Resolve, then commit.
2. **Registers, through their doors.**
   - `node scripts/wiring-census.mjs` (then `--check`).
   - `node scripts/generate-dossier-state-prose.mjs` (then `--check`).
   - `node scripts/check-writer-reach.mjs --write`.
   - `npm run build:edge-shared`: keep only the bundles whose sourceHash moved.
   - On a clean tree: `node scripts/check-observed-shape-readers.mjs --write`.
   - On a clean tree: `LIGHTING_CENSUS_REFREEZE='…' LIGHTING_CENSUS_NOTE='…' npx vitest run tests/lint/sovereigntyLightingContract.walker.test.js`, then re-run it plain until green.
3. **Stage and measure.**
   - `sh scripts/audit/landing-stage.sh build <merge> /tmp/claude-0/stages/sN`.
   - `measure /tmp/claude-0/stages/sN /tmp/claude-0/stages/mN /tmp/claude-0/stages/m<prev>`. The last measurement is `m16`.
4. **Sign** with the `/tmp/claude-0/rec/` helpers (`sign.py`, `byhand.py`, `fielddiff.mjs`).
   - `/tmp` does not survive the container. A new seat rebuilds `m16` by staging 9a88d6eba.
   - Steps:
     - Run `node scripts/audit/landing-act.mjs run <cfg>`.
     - Re-key the fork-census control in the stage.
     - Run `landing-stage.sh suites <stage>`.
     - Run `landing-act.mjs transplant <cfg>`.
     - Bump the roster tree.
   - Door row counts: witness predictedRows 15, letter 4.
5. **Check.**
   - `sh scripts/gate-tail.sh npm run typecheck:ratchet`.
   - The governing files: every test naming a src module the lane changed, through `sh scripts/gate-tail.sh npx vitest run --maxWorkers=2 <files>`.
6. **Push.** `git fetch` and check ancestry: a moved remote is a STOP. Then three separate calls: `git add`, `git commit -q -F <msg>`, `git push --no-verify -u origin lane/integration-2026-11-07`.
7. **The 1,000 soak** runs at the closing only.

`fielddiff.mjs <treeA> <treeB>`: every golden-corpus settlement, diffed by leaf path; prints the rows moved per top-level field, the field paths, and every non-string leaf move.
```js
// usage: node fielddiff.mjs <treeA> <treeB> [--rows N]  — diffs every golden-corpus settlement leaf by path.
// Prints per top-level field: rows moved; and every NON-STRING leaf change (type or value) with examples.
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
import { resolve } from 'node:path';
const [a, b] = process.argv.slice(2).map((p) => resolve(p));
const load = async (root) => ({
  gen: (await import(pathToFileURL(`${root}/src/generators/generateSettlementPipeline.js`).href)).generateSettlementPipeline,
  corp: await import(pathToFileURL(`${root}/tests/helpers/goldenMasterCorpus.js`).href),
});
const A = await load(a); const B = await load(b);
const flat = (v, p, out) => {
  if (v && typeof v === 'object') { const ks = Object.keys(v); if (!ks.length) out.set(p, Array.isArray(v) ? '[]' : '{}'); for (const k of ks) flat(v[k], `${p}.${k}`, out); }
  else out.set(p, v);
  return out;
};
const top = {}; const nonString = {}; let rowsMoved = 0; const fieldSet = new Set();
for (const config of A.corp.goldenCorpus()) {
  const { _seed, ...cfg } = config;
  const sa = A.gen(cfg, null, { seed: _seed, customContent: {} });
  const sb = B.gen(cfg, null, { seed: _seed, customContent: {} });
  if (JSON.stringify(sa) === JSON.stringify(sb)) continue;
  rowsMoved++;
  const fa = flat(sa, '', new Map()); const fb = flat(sb, '', new Map());
  const keys = new Set([...fa.keys(), ...fb.keys()]);
  const tops = new Set();
  for (const k of keys) {
    const x = fa.get(k), y = fb.get(k);
    if (x === y) continue;
    const t = k.split('.')[1]; tops.add(t);
    const norm = k.replace(/\.\d+(?=\.|$)/g, '.#');
    fieldSet.add(norm);
    if (!(typeof x === 'string' && typeof y === 'string')) {
      const e = (nonString[norm] ??= { n: 0, ex: [] }); e.n++; if (e.ex.length < 3) e.ex.push([A.corp.keyOf(config), x, y]);
    }
  }
  for (const t of tops) top[t] = (top[t] ?? 0) + 1;
}
console.log(JSON.stringify({ rowsMoved, top, fieldPaths: [...fieldSet], nonString }, null, 1));
```

`herald-row-text.mjs <tree> <rowId> <years> <out.json>`: the herald desk golden row's CONTENT (what contentSha256 hashes), for a text diff (run from inside the stage).
```js
#!/usr/bin/env node
// node herald-row-text.mjs <tree> <rowId> <years> <out.json>: the herald desk golden row's CONTENT (what contentSha256 hashes), for a text diff.
import { writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { resolve } from 'node:path';
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
const [tree, rowId, years, out] = process.argv.slice(2);
const root = resolve(tree); const load = (rel) => import(pathToFileURL(`${root}/${rel}`).href);
const { buildHeraldFeed } = await load('src/components/map/heraldFeed.js');
const { advanceReaderCampaign, composeReaderRegion, READER_CORPUS_ROSTER } = await load('scripts/review/readerCorpus.mjs');
const rosterRow = READER_CORPUS_ROSTER.find((r) => r.campaignId === rowId);
const { campaign, saves } = composeReaderRegion(rosterRow);
const advanced = await advanceReaderCampaign({ campaign, saves, years: Number(years), seed: rosterRow.seed });
const feed = buildHeraldFeed(advanced.campaign, { lens: 'campaign' });
const items = [];
for (const [section, list] of Object.entries(feed.bySection)) for (const item of list) items.push({ section, item });
items.sort((a, b) => (a.item.id < b.item.id ? -1 : a.item.id > b.item.id ? 1 : 0));
const content = items.map(({ item }) => ({ id: item.id, kind: item.kind, headline: item.headline, summary: item.summary,
  severity: item.severity, major: item.major, tick: item.tick, reasons: item.reasons, affectedIds: item.affectedIds, provenance: item.provenance }));
writeFileSync(out, JSON.stringify(content, null, 1) + '\n');
console.log(JSON.stringify({ items: content.length }));
```

`pn.mjs <out.json>`: the prose-numerics live scan, which this file's own test runs; the output is the regenerated baseline (run from the worktree root).
```js
import { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, relative } from 'node:path';
const ROOT = process.cwd();
const { scanProseNumericsSource } = await import(join(ROOT, 'tests/helpers/proseNumericsWalk.js'));
const GEN = new Set(['src/components/home/landingFixture.js']);
const walk = (d, out = []) => { for (const e of readdirSync(d)) { const a = join(d, e); if (statSync(a).isDirectory()) walk(a, out); else if (/\.(?:js|jsx)$/.test(e)) { const r = relative(ROOT, a).replace(/\\/g, '/'); if (!GEN.has(r)) out.push(a); } } return out; };
const hits = []; for (const a of walk(join(ROOT, 'src')).sort()) { const path = relative(ROOT, a).replace(/\\/g, '/'); hits.push(...scanProseNumericsSource({ source: readFileSync(a, 'utf8'), path }).hits); }
const cats = {}; for (const h of hits) cats[h.category] = (cats[h.category] ?? 0) + 1;
writeFileSync(process.argv[2], JSON.stringify(hits, null, 2) + '\n');
console.log(JSON.stringify({ total: hits.length, cats }));
```

`govfiles.sh <range>`: the governing test files (every test naming a changed src module by basename).
```sh
#!/bin/sh
# usage: govfiles.sh <range> [extra-range...] -> prints test files naming any changed src module (by basename import)
cd /home/user/wt-merge2
for r in "$@"; do git diff --name-only "$r" -- src; done | sort -u | while read f; do
  b=$(basename "$f"); b=${b%.*}
  grep -rlE "[/'\"]${b}(\.(js|jsx|ts|mjs))?['\"]" tests --include='*.js' --include='*.jsx' --include='*.mjs' --include='*.ts' 2>/dev/null
done | sort -u
```

`roster.py <LANE LABEL> <lane record path> <key>`: the dated-roster conflict resolved as a UNION, `dated` = ours + 1, with the walker header line (run inside the merge).
```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# usage: roster.py <lane-label> <lane-record-path> <note-key-suffix>
# resolves the two roster conflicts as a UNION: ours then theirs; dated = ours+1; our merge note appended; header line prepended.
import re,sys,json
lane,rec,key=sys.argv[1:4]
B='tests/lint/.source-citation-baseline.json'; W='tests/lint/sourceCitationIntegrity.walker.test.js'
CONF=re.compile(r'<<<<<<< [^\n]*\n(.*?)=======\n(.*?)>>>>>>> [^\n]*\n',re.S)
t=open(B).read()
blocks=CONF.findall(t)
m=re.search(r'"dated": (\d+)',blocks[0][0]); ours=int(m.group(1)); new=ours+1
def res(mo):
    o,th=mo.group(1),mo.group(2)
    if '"dated":' in o: return re.sub(r'"dated": \d+','"dated": %d'%new,o)
    o=o.rstrip('\n'); th=th.rstrip('\n')
    if not o.endswith(','): o+=','
    note='    "_rosterGrowth2026-11-09-style-2-merge-%s": "THE UNION AT THE %s MERGE (STYLE-2-MERGE-2, session session_01FbPLbc8gtufq3JftaYsRGV): both sides\' notes kept; `dated` %d -> %d (%s, the lane\'s record, born dated)."'%(key,lane,ours,new,rec)
    th_c=th if th.endswith(',') else th+','
    return o+'\n'+th_c+'\n'+note+'\n'
t2=CONF.sub(res,t)
assert '<<<<<<<' not in t2
json.loads(t2)
open(B,'w').write(t2)
w=open(W).read()
def resw(mo):
    o=mo.group(1)
    hm=re.match(r'( \*   dated \()(\d+)(\)  an ISO date in the filename \()(.*)',o,re.S)
    assert hm and int(hm.group(2))==ours, o[:80]
    return '%s%d%s2026-11-09, STYLE-2-MERGE-2 at the %s merge, %d -> %d: the union with the lane\'s record %s; %s'%(hm.group(1),new,hm.group(3),lane,ours,new,rec,hm.group(4))
w2=CONF.sub(resw,w)
assert '<<<<<<<' not in w2
open(W,'w').write(w2)
print('dated',ours,'->',new)
```

`stagepins.py <cfg.json> [--control <hash>]` (run inside the stage after `landing-act.mjs run`): the gm suite's header block, worldFactRederive's A7 pin, and with `--control` the fork census control hash (read it from `npx vitest run tests/generators/generationForkCensus.test.js`'s `Received:` line). Commit in the stage, then `landing-stage.sh suites`.
```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# usage (inside the stage): stagepins.py <cfg.json> [--control <newhash>]
# Writes the gm suite's header block, worldFactRederive's A7 pin, and (with --control) the fork census control hash.
import json,sys,re
cfg=json.load(open(sys.argv[1])); g=cfg['gmPins']
n=cfg['n']; recbase=cfg['record'].split('/')[-1]
# 1. the gm suite header block, newest first
p='tests/property/generatorGoldenMaster.test.js'; t=open(p).read()
anchor=' * 2026-11-09 — STYLE-2-MERGE RECORD '
i=t.index(anchor)
block=(' * 2026-11-09 — STYLE-2-MERGE RECORD %s, %s, THROUGH THE SIGNED DOOR (ODQ §934.105, THE STYLE WAVE\'S MERGE, STYLE-2-MERGE;\n'
       ' *   record %s; the cloud chair signs under the owner\'s delegation, vetoable).\n'
       ' *   %s against record %s\'s post-state. THE CAUSE, ONE (first-parent step %s): %s FIELD-DIFFED: %s\n'
       ' *   Written from the staged tree S%s (the tree of %s with the door paths carried from the branch). %s.. -> %s..\n'
       ' *\n')%(n,g['titleCaps'],cfg['record'],g['rowsText'],g['prevRecord'],cfg['step'][:9],g['causeShort'],g['fieldDiff'],n,cfg['step'][:9],g['from'][:8],g['to'][:8])
if ('RECORD %s,'%n) not in t:
    t=t[:i]+block+t[i:]; open(p,'w').write(t)
# 2. worldFactRederive's A7 pin
p='tests/domain/worldFactRederive.test.js'; t=open(p).read()
if ("      .toBe('%s');"%g['to']) in t: t=None
if t is not None:
    old="      .toBe('%s');"%g['from']
    assert t.count(old)==1, 'A7 pin not found once'
    pos=t.index(old)
    exp="    expect(shaOfFile(GOLDEN), 'tests/fixtures/generator-golden-master.json moved')\n"
    assert t[:pos].endswith(exp)
    pre=t[:pos-len(exp)]
    lines=('    // Record %s of the style wave\'s merge, %s (%s), from the stage at %s:\n'
           '    // the generator master %s.. -> %s.. (%s).\n')%(n,g['titleLower'],recbase,cfg['step'][:9],g['from'][:8],g['to'][:8],g['rowsShort'])
    t=pre+lines+exp+"      .toBe('%s');"%g['to']+t[pos+len(old):]
    open(p,'w').write(t)
# 3. the fork census control hash
if '--control' in sys.argv:
    new=sys.argv[sys.argv.index('--control')+1]
    p='tests/generators/generationForkCensus.test.js'; t=open(p).read()
    m=re.search(r"const GOLDEN_CONTROL_HASH = '([0-9a-f]{64})';",t); old=m.group(1)
    if old!=new:
        note=g['controlNote'].replace('{new}',new[:8])
        t=t[:m.start()]+note+"const GOLDEN_CONTROL_HASH = '%s';"%new+t[m.end():]
        open(p,'w').write(t)
    print('control',old[:8],'->',new[:8])
print('pins written')
```

`heralddiff.py <before.json> <after.json> <lane-added-lines>`: the herald row's rendered diff (from `herald-row-text.mjs` at each stage); lists any new string with no 18-character run in the lane's added source lines (`git diff <base> <lane tip> -- src | grep '^+'`) — read every one listed by hand.
```python
import json,sys,difflib
a=json.load(open(sys.argv[1])); b=json.load(open(sys.argv[2])); add=open(sys.argv[3]).read()
A={x['id']:x for x in a}; B={x['id']:x for x in b}
assert set(A)==set(B), 'ids differ'
nontext=0; moved=0; new=set(); old=set()
T=('headline','summary','reasons')
def strs(x):
    out=[]
    for f in T:
        v=x.get(f); out+= v if isinstance(v,list) else [v]
    return [s for s in out if isinstance(s,str)]
for k in A:
    x,y=A[k],B[k]
    if any(json.dumps(x.get(f))!=json.dumps(y.get(f)) for f in x if f not in T): nontext+=1
    if any(json.dumps(x.get(f))!=json.dumps(y.get(f)) for f in T):
        moved+=1; sx=set(strs(x)); sy=set(strs(y)); new|=sy-sx; old|=sx-sy
def lcs(s):
    m=difflib.SequenceMatcher(None,s,add,autojunk=False).find_longest_match(0,len(s),0,len(add))
    return m.size
weak=[s for s in sorted(new) if lcs(s)<min(18,len(s))]
print(json.dumps({'items':len(B),'nonTextMoves':nontext,'itemsTextMoved':moved,'newStrings':len(new),'untraced':len(weak)}))
for s in weak[:20]: print('  UNTRACED:',s[:200])
```

## Appendix C. The fourth seat's helper (outside scripts/; recreate it from this quote)

`compose3.py <file> [--apply] [--keep-ours-on-overlap N,M]` (run on a file checked out with `git checkout --conflict=diff3 -- <file>`): a line-level three-way compose of each conflict block; blocks whose two sides touch DISJOINT base lines are composed, overlapping blocks are printed with the three texts and never guessed (they are the STOPs). Used at point 24 on eight source files.
```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# usage: compose3.py <file> [--apply] [--keep-ours-on-overlap N,M]
# Reads a file in diff3 conflict form (<<<<<<< ours / ||||||| base / ======= / >>>>>>> theirs).
# For each block: a line-level 3-way compose — base->ours and base->theirs edits applied together
# when they touch DISJOINT base line ranges (an insertion counts as touching its anchor point).
# Overlapping blocks (two lanes rewrote the same base line) are REPORTED with the three texts, never guessed;
# with --keep-ours-on-overlap the listed block numbers (1-based) take ours whole (the STOP's default).
# STYLE-2-MERGE-4.
import re, sys, difflib
path = sys.argv[1]; apply = '--apply' in sys.argv
keep = set()
if '--keep-ours-on-overlap' in sys.argv:
    keep = {int(x) for x in sys.argv[sys.argv.index('--keep-ours-on-overlap') + 1].split(',')}
t = open(path).read()
C = re.compile(r'<<<<<<< [^\n]*\n(.*?)\|\|\|\|\|\|\| [^\n]*\n(.*?)=======\n(.*?)>>>>>>> [^\n]*\n', re.S)

def edits(base, side):
    sm = difflib.SequenceMatcher(None, base, side, autojunk=False)
    return [(i1, i2, side[j1:j2]) for tag, i1, i2, j1, j2 in sm.get_opcodes() if tag != 'equal']

def touch(e):
    i1, i2, _ = e
    return (i1, i2) if i2 > i1 else (i1 - 0.5, i1 + 0.5)  # an insertion touches its anchor

def overlap(a, b):
    return a[0] < b[1] and b[0] < a[1]

n = [0]; report = []
def res(m):
    n[0] += 1; k = n[0]
    o, b, th = (x.splitlines(keepends=True) for x in m.groups())
    eo, et = edits(b, o), edits(b, th)
    clash = [(x, y) for x in eo for y in et if overlap(touch(x), touch(y)) and not (x[0] == y[0] and x[1] == y[1] and x[2] == y[2])]
    if clash:
        report.append((k, ''.join(o), ''.join(b), ''.join(th)))
        if k in keep: return ''.join(o)
        return m.group(0)
    out = []; allE = sorted([(x[0], x[1], x[2], 'o') for x in eo] + [(y[0], y[1], y[2], 't') for y in et if y not in [(x[0], x[1], x[2]) for x in eo]])
    i = 0
    for i1, i2, rep, _ in allE:
        out += b[i:i1]; out += rep; i = max(i, i2)
    out += b[i:]
    return ''.join(out)
t2 = C.sub(res, t)
print(f'{path}: {n[0]} block(s), {len(report)} overlapping')
for k, o, b, th in report:
    print(f'--- block {k} OVERLAP\n[ours]\n{o}[base]\n{b}[theirs]\n{th}')
if apply:
    open(path, 'w').write(t2)
    print('applied; markers left:', t2.count('<<<<<<<'))
```

## Appendix D. The fifth seat's helper (outside scripts/; recreate it from this quote)

`rcpt.mjs <treeA> <treeB>`: the generation coherence receipt diffed stage to stage over the golden corpus — rows moved, findings dropped (each new findings list must be an order-kept subsequence of the old), status flips, and anything else that moved (`other`, which must be 0 for a removal-only receipt flip). Used at point 25.
```js
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
const [a,b]=process.argv.slice(2);
const L=async r=>({gen:(await import(pathToFileURL(`${r}/src/generators/generateSettlementPipeline.js`).href)).generateSettlementPipeline,corp:await import(pathToFileURL(`${r}/tests/helpers/goldenMasterCorpus.js`).href)});
const A=await L(a),B=await L(b);
let rows=0,removedOnly=0,other=[],dropped=0,statusFlip={};
for(const c of A.corp.goldenCorpus()){const{_seed,...cfg}=c;
 const ra=A.gen(cfg,null,{seed:_seed,customContent:{}}).generationCoherenceReceipt, rb=B.gen(cfg,null,{seed:_seed,customContent:{}}).generationCoherenceReceipt;
 if(JSON.stringify(ra)===JSON.stringify(rb)) continue; rows++;
 const ka=Object.keys(ra).join(),kb=Object.keys(rb).join(); if(ka!==kb){other.push(['keys',A.corp.keyOf(c)]);continue;}
 for(const k of Object.keys(ra)) if(k!=='judgments'&&JSON.stringify(ra[k])!==JSON.stringify(rb[k])) other.push([k,A.corp.keyOf(c),ra[k],rb[k]]);
 if(ra.judgments.length!==rb.judgments.length){other.push(['jlen',A.corp.keyOf(c)]);continue;}
 ra.judgments.forEach((ja,i)=>{const jb=rb.judgments[i];
  for(const k of new Set([...Object.keys(ja),...Object.keys(jb)])) if(k!=='findings'&&k!=='status'&&JSON.stringify(ja[k])!==JSON.stringify(jb[k])) other.push(['j.'+k,A.corp.keyOf(c)]);
  if(ja.status!==jb.status){const t=ja.status+'->'+jb.status;statusFlip[t]=(statusFlip[t]??0)+1;}
  const fa=(ja.findings??[]).map(x=>JSON.stringify(x)),fb=(jb.findings??[]).map(x=>JSON.stringify(x));
  let p=0;for(const x of fa){if(p<fb.length&&fb[p]===x)p++;}
  if(p!==fb.length) other.push(['findings-not-subsequence',A.corp.keyOf(c),fa,fb]); else dropped+=fa.length-fb.length;
 });}
console.log(JSON.stringify({rows,dropped,statusFlip,other:other.length,ex:other.slice(0,4)},null,1));
```

## Appendix F. The seventh seat's helpers (were in /home/user/rec7/; quoted whole so the eighth seat can recreate them)

`gate.sh` — the point gate (`BARE=1` runs the build with NODE_OPTIONS unset; `vd` adds verify:dist).
```sh
#!/bin/sh
# usage: gate.sh <n> <govfile> [vd] — STYLE-2-MERGE-7: build, boot smoke, strict, typecheck, tests/lint, tests/scripts, governing files, (vd: verify:dist)
n=$1; gov=$2; S=/home/user/rec7/gate$n.sum; L=/home/user/rec7/g$n; : > $S
```

`roster.py` — the dated-roster union.
```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# usage: roster.py <lane-label> <lane-record-path> <note-key-suffix> — the dated-roster conflict as a UNION (STYLE-2-MERGE-7)
import re,sys,json
lane,rec,key=sys.argv[1:4]
B='tests/lint/.source-citation-baseline.json'; W='tests/lint/sourceCitationIntegrity.walker.test.js'
CONF=re.compile(r'<<<<<<< [^\n]*\n(.*?)=======\n(.*?)>>>>>>> [^\n]*\n',re.S)
t=open(B).read()
blocks=CONF.findall(t)
ours=None
for o,_ in blocks:
    m=re.search(r'"dated": (\d+)',o)
    if m: ours=int(m.group(1))
new=ours+1
def res(mo):
    o,th=mo.group(1),mo.group(2)
    if '"dated":' in o: return re.sub(r'"dated": \d+','"dated": %d'%new,o)
    o=o.rstrip('\n'); th=th.rstrip('\n')
    if not o.endswith(','): o+=','
    note='    "_rosterGrowth2026-11-11-style-2-merge-%s": "THE UNION AT THE %s MERGE (STYLE-2-MERGE-7, session session_01FbPLbf9PtvYaSbTPWUnwoY): both sides\' notes kept; `dated` %d -> %d (%s, the lane\'s record, born dated)."'%(key,lane,ours,new,rec)
    th_c=th if th.endswith(',') else th+','
    return o+'\n'+th_c+'\n'+note+',\n'
t2=CONF.sub(res,t)
assert '<<<<<<<' not in t2
json.loads(t2)
open(B,'w').write(t2)
w=open(W).read()
def resw(mo):
    o=mo.group(1)
    hm=re.match(r'( \*   dated \()(\d+)(\)  an ISO date in the filename \()(.*)',o,re.S)
    assert hm and int(hm.group(2))==ours, o[:80]
    return '%s%d%s2026-11-11, STYLE-2-MERGE-7 at the %s merge, %d -> %d: the union with the lane\'s record %s; %s'%(hm.group(1),new,hm.group(3),lane,ours,new,rec,hm.group(4))
w2=CONF.sub(resw,w)
assert '<<<<<<<' not in w2
open(W,'w').write(w2)
print('dated',ours,'->',new)
```

```python
import re,sys,json
n,rec,lane=sys.argv[1:4]
SEAT='STYLE-2-MERGE-7, session session_01FbPLbf9PtvYaSbTPWUnwoY'
B='tests/lint/.source-citation-baseline.json'; W='tests/lint/sourceCitationIntegrity.walker.test.js'

t=t[:m.start()]+'"tree": %d\n'%new+t[m.end():]
ms=list(re.finditer(r'\n(    "_rosterGrowth[^\n]*")(,?)\n',t)); last=ms[-1]
note='    "_rosterGrowth2026-11-11-style-2-merge-record-%s": "ONE document JOINED the roster by being BORN into it (%s): `tree` %d -> %d, %s, record %s, born into the tree class; measured once by this walker; nothing changed class."'%(n,SEAT,old,new,rec,n)
t=t[:last.start()]+'\n'+last.group(1)+',\n'+note+(last.group(2))+'\n'+t[last.end():]

a=' *   tree (%d)   docs/review-r2/**, docs/shift-records/** ('%old

open(W,'w').write(w); print(old,'->',new)
```

`cellattr.py` — act 2: per-point rows/cells moved (reads cellpts.txt = the base, each merge's first parent, each merge).
```python
# act 2's attribution: rows (of 1,050) and cells moved at each point boundary (STYLE-2-MERGE-7)
import json,subprocess,collections
pts=[l.strip() for l in open('/home/user/rec7/cellpts.txt')]
def load(s):
    d=json.load(open(f'/home/user/stages/cells/{s}.json'))
    cells={c['cell']:json.dumps(c,sort_keys=True) for c in d['cells']}
    return d['rows'],cells
merges=subprocess.check_output(['git','-C','/home/user/wt-merge7','log','--first-parent','--merges','--format=%h %P','--abbrev=9','bd07005b9..4ca5cee57']).decode().split('\n')
merges=[m.split() for m in merges if m][::-1]
subj={}
for m in merges: subj[m[0]]=subprocess.check_output(['git','-C','/home/user/wt-merge7','log','-1','--format=%s',m[0]]).decode().strip()[:80]
prev='bd07005b9'; R0,C0=load(prev)
g=json.load(open('/home/user/wt-merge7/tests/fixtures/dossier-prose-manifest-golden.json'))['rows']
print('base rows == committed golden:', R0==g, 'cells', len(C0))
def diff(a,b):
    (ra,ca),(rb,cb)=a,b
    rows=sum(1 for k in set(ra)|set(rb) if ra.get(k)!=rb.get(k))
    cm=sum(1 for k in set(ca)&set(cb) if ca[k]!=cb[k]); add=len(set(cb)-set(ca)); rem=len(set(ca)-set(cb))
    return rows,cm,add,rem
out=[]
cur=(R0,C0); curs=prev
for m,p1,p2 in merges:
    p1s=p1[:9]
    A=load(p1s); 
    if p1s!=curs:
        d=diff(cur,A)
        if any(d): out.append(('between '+curs+'..'+p1s,)+d)
    B=load(m); d=diff(A,B); out.append((m+' '+subj[m],)+d)
    cur=B; curs=m
for o in out: print(o)
tot=diff((R0,C0),cur); print('base -> 4ca5cee57 total rows,moved,added,removed:',tot)
```

`hl-measure.mjs` — act 4: the headline and prose-family contracts measured through their own functions.
```js
// STYLE-2-MERGE-7 closing act 4: the headline contract's live figures, through the walker's own functions.
import { writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
const R = (process.env.TREE ?? '/home/user/wt-merge7') + '/';
const { buildObservedCorpus, OBSERVED_SCALAR_FIELDS } = await import(R + 'scripts/lib/observed-shape-corpus.mjs');
const H = await import(R + 'scripts/lib/news-headline-contract.mjs');
const { reconstructWizardNewsIntroductions } = await import(R + 'scripts/lib/news-voice-contract.mjs');
const { APPLIED_HEADLINE_REWRITES } = await import(R + 'src/domain/worldPulse/worldPulseFeedCuration.js');
const corpus = await buildObservedCorpus({ scalarFields: OBSERVED_SCALAR_FIELDS });
const rec = reconstructWizardNewsIntroductions(corpus.scalarObservations);
const rows = H.deriveNewsAddressRows(rec.entries);
const raw = H.selectPersistedHeadlineRows(corpus.scalarObservations);
const out = { rows, rowsSha256: H.addressRowsSha256(rows), raw: H.rawHeadlineLivenessOf(raw), liveness: H.analyzeHeadlineRewriteLiveness(raw, APPLIED_HEADLINE_REWRITES) };
writeFileSync(process.argv[2], JSON.stringify(out, null, 1));
console.log(rows.length, out.rowsSha256);
// the prose-family contract's live figures (closing act 4), same corpus
try {
  const P = await import((process.env.PFLIB ?? R) + 'scripts/lib/prose-family-contract.mjs');
  const live = P.deriveProseFamilyContract(corpus.scalarObservations, corpus.scalarMeta);
  const { writeFileSync: w } = await import('node:fs');
  w(process.argv[2].replace(/\.json$/, '.pf.json'), JSON.stringify({ corpus: live.corpus, familyTotals: live.familyTotals, totals: live.totals, rows: live.rows, rowsSha256: P.proseFamilyRowsSha256(live.rows), rowsBytes: JSON.stringify(live.rows).length }, null, 1));
  console.log('pf', live.totals, JSON.stringify(live.rows).length);
} catch (e) { console.log('pf-failed', e.message); }
```

`distm.mjs` — act 7: first paint and worker bytes of a built tree.
```js
// STYLE-2-MERGE-7 act 7: first-paint closure (raw/gzip-9/brotli-11, the vendorPdfLazy method) and the worker bundle bytes of <tree>/dist
import { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { gzipSync, brotliCompressSync, constants } from 'node:zlib';
const dist = join(process.argv[2], 'dist'), A = join(dist, 'assets');
const spec = (c) => { const s = new Set(); let m; const f = /\bfrom\s*["'](\.\/[^"']+\.js)["']/g, b = /(?:^|[;}])import\s*["'](\.\/[^"']+\.js)["']/g;
  while ((m = f.exec(c))) s.add(m[1].replace('./', '')); while ((m = b.exec(c))) s.add(m[1].replace('./', '')); return [...s]; };
const entry = readFileSync(join(dist, 'index.html'), 'utf8').match(/<script[^>]*type="module"[^>]*src="\/assets\/([^"]+)"/)[1];
const seen = new Set([entry]); const q = [entry];
while (q.length) { const f = q.shift(); for (const d of spec(readFileSync(join(A, f), 'utf8'))) if (!seen.has(d)) { seen.add(d); q.push(d); } }
let raw = 0, gz = 0, br = 0;
for (const f of [...seen].sort()) { const b = readFileSync(join(A, f)); raw += statSync(join(A, f)).size; gz += gzipSync(b, { level: 9 }).length; br += brotliCompressSync(b, { params: { [constants.BROTLI_PARAM_QUALITY]: 11 } }).length; }
const w = readdirSync(A).filter((f) => /^generation\.worker-.*\.js$/.test(f));
console.log(JSON.stringify({ raw, gz, br, worker: w.map((f) => statSync(join(A, f)).size), files: seen.size }));
```

```js
    'GONE BY RE-HOMING, attributed: GREEN-TIP b46048140 moved the one read into src/generators/foodLand.js (its NEW twin). 1 -> 0.'],
  'src/domain/worldPulse/causeLifecycle.js\u0000secondaryAffiliation on npcs': [1, 0,
    'GONE BY REPAIR, attributed: PULSE-SITES 6 (5153945c2) removed the cause lifecycle\'s dead `secondaryAffiliation` fallback (STYLE-2-MERGE point 36). The row the walker\'s THE ERASURE GUARD names; a lawful repair, owed here (the chair, 08:1x). 1 -> 0.'],
  'src/domain/entities/propagate.js\u0000linkedFactionIds on npcs': [1, 0,
    'GONE BY REPAIR, attributed: GREEN-TIP P3 (44b37a520, STYLE-2-MERGE point 36) re-pointed propagation\'s NPC hop from the retired `linkedFactionIds` to the card\'s memberships — a pure re-point; the read left. 1 -> 0.'],
}));
const EXPECTED_SAME = 1526;
const EXPECTED_DELTA = ['package.json', 'scripts/check-observed-shape-readers.mjs',
  'scripts/lib/observed-shape-baseline.mjs', 'scripts/migrate-observed-shape-readers.mjs'];
const EXPECTED_UNSCANNED = [
  'src/data/dossierCausalProse.generated.js',
  'src/data/dossierStateProse/defense.generated.js',
  'src/data/dossierStateProse/general.generated.js',
  'src/data/dossierStateProse/power.generated.js',
  'src/data/dossierStateProse/stressors.generated.js',
  'src/data/dossierStateProse/warFaith.generated.js',
  'src/domain/compendium/generated/compendiumData.generated.js',
  'src/domain/content/customContentManifest.generated.js',
];
const EXPECTED_CORPUS_MOVED = ['shapeCount: 1734 -> 1740'];

const same = report.predecessorRows.filter((row) => row.reconciliation === 'same');
const moved = report.predecessorRows.filter((row) => row.reconciliation !== 'same');
if (same.length !== EXPECTED_SAME) refuse(`SAME ${same.length} != ${EXPECTED_SAME}`);
if (moved.length !== EXPECTED.size) refuse(`moved ${moved.length} != ${EXPECTED.size}`);
const notes = new Map();
for (const row of moved) {
  const key = `${row.address.file}\u0000${row.address.identity}`;
  const want = EXPECTED.get(key);
  if (!want) refuse(`unexpected moved row ${row.address.file} / ${row.address.identity}`);
  if (row.predecessorCount !== want[0] || row.legacyCount !== want[1]) {
    refuse(`row ${row.address.file} / ${row.address.identity} is ${row.predecessorCount} -> ${row.legacyCount}, expected ${want[0]} -> ${want[1]}`);
  }
  notes.set(row.rowId, want[2]);
}
if (report.rows.length) refuse(`site-migration rows present: ${report.rows.length}`);
const st = report.scannerTransition;
if (!st || JSON.stringify(st.modifiedPaths) !== JSON.stringify(EXPECTED_DELTA)) {
  refuse(`scanner transition ${JSON.stringify(st?.modifiedPaths)}`);
}
if (JSON.stringify(st.unscannedMovement?.changedPaths) !== JSON.stringify(EXPECTED_UNSCANNED)) {
  refuse(`unscanned movement ${JSON.stringify(st.unscannedMovement?.changedPaths)}`);
}
if (JSON.stringify(report.corpusCompatibility?.moved) !== JSON.stringify(EXPECTED_CORPUS_MOVED)) {
  refuse(`corpus movement ${JSON.stringify(report.corpusCompatibility?.moved)}`);
}
const sameById = new Map(same.map((row) => [row.rowId, row]));
const TRANSITION_NOTE = 'the schema-39 -> 40 rung\'s four detector-input paths at the style wave\'s final tip: the three bookkeeping paths of act 1 (8ad1df506; the live validator to 40, the retired-39 constant, the rung and the one declaration) and package.json, CI-FIT 001fc7e00 (build heap 4096; merged at point 40, 4ca5cee57), named in the delta law at 0821e441f. The UNSCANNED inputs moved, eight generated execution inputs, each attributed: SW-8 C8 e63c6aae6 (dossierCausalProse, dossierStateProse defense/general/power/stressors/warFaith); SW-7 POWER-SIDE e76cb0ab7, 7c96f27aa, c1d64ef0f (power.generated.js, point 16); SW-8 C2 a46d1ba8b, C6 1e15a3c10, C7 0da9265f9 and LIBRARY-SIMPLE 2920100e0 (compendiumData.generated.js); SW-8 C7 0da9265f9 (customContentManifest.generated.js). The corpus moved shapeCount 1734 -> 1740 (originCount 14344 -> 14782, transitionCount 22053 -> 22772): the simulation batch, point 36 (GREEN-TIP 6eeb5f82c + PULSE-SITES 47de1b76a), measured there by writer reach. The corpus DEFINITION is unchanged.';
const decisions = template.decisions.map((decision) => {
  if (decision.subject === 'scanner-transition') return { ...decision, decision: 'accept', note: TRANSITION_NOTE };
  if (decision.subject !== 'predecessor-reconciliation') refuse(`unexpected subject ${decision.subject}`);
  if (notes.has(decision.rowId)) return { ...decision, decision: 'accept', note: notes.get(decision.rowId) };
  const row = sameById.get(decision.rowId);
  if (!row) refuse(`template row ${decision.rowId} not in report`);
  return { ...decision, decision: 'accept', note: `SAME against the committed schema-39 register: ${row.legacyCount} read(s).` };
});
if (decisions.length !== EXPECTED_SAME + EXPECTED.size + 1) refuse(`decisions ${decisions.length}`);
writeFileSync(outPath, `${JSON.stringify({ ...template, decisions }, null, 2)}\n`, { flag: 'wx' });
console.log(`review written: ${decisions.length} decisions (${EXPECTED_SAME} SAME, ${EXPECTED.size} moved, 1 transition)`);
```

`build-review41.mjs` — act 5: the rung-41 review builder.
```js
// Scratch review builder for OSR rung 41 act 2. REFUSES (exit 1, nothing written) unless the
// reconciliation is ALL SAME (1532 rows, including `outcome on outcome` at newsBody.js at 1),
// the transition EXACTLY the three bookkeeping paths, no unscanned movement, no corpus movement.
// usage: node build-review41.mjs <report.json> <template.json> <out-review.json>
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';
const [reportPath, templatePath, outPath] = process.argv.slice(2);
const report = JSON.parse(readFileSync(reportPath, 'utf8'));
const template = JSON.parse(readFileSync(templatePath, 'utf8'));
const refuse = (why) => { console.error(`REFUSED: ${why}`); process.exit(1); };
if (existsSync(outPath)) refuse(`${outPath} exists`);
const EXPECTED_SAME = 1532;
const DELTA = ['scripts/check-observed-shape-readers.mjs', 'scripts/lib/observed-shape-baseline.mjs',
  'scripts/migrate-observed-shape-readers.mjs'];
const moved = report.predecessorRows.filter((row) => row.reconciliation !== 'same');
if (moved.length) refuse(`moved rows: ${moved.map((r) => `${r.address.file} / ${r.address.identity} ${r.predecessorCount} -> ${r.legacyCount}`).join('; ')}`);
if (report.predecessorRows.length !== EXPECTED_SAME) refuse(`SAME ${report.predecessorRows.length} != ${EXPECTED_SAME}`);
const target = report.predecessorRows.filter((r) => r.address.file === 'src/domain/display/newsBody.js'
  && r.address.identity === 'outcome on outcome');
if (target.length !== 1 || target[0].legacyCount !== 1) refuse('the declared row is not newsBody.js `outcome on outcome` at 1');
if (report.rows.length) refuse(`site rows ${report.rows.length}`);
const st = report.scannerTransition;
if (JSON.stringify(st?.modifiedPaths) !== JSON.stringify(DELTA)) refuse(`transition ${JSON.stringify(st?.modifiedPaths)}`);
if (st.unscannedMovement) refuse(`unscanned movement ${JSON.stringify(st.unscannedMovement.changedPaths)}`);
if ((report.corpusCompatibility?.moved || []).length) refuse(`corpus moved ${JSON.stringify(report.corpusCompatibility.moved)}`);
const byId = new Map(report.predecessorRows.map((row) => [row.rowId, row]));
const TAGGED = 'SAME count, TAGGED under the schema-41 declaration (the chair\'s FINAL RULING 21:2x, STYLE-2-MERGE closing act 5): `outcome on outcome`, the first played-worlds writer — src/domain/worldPulse/convergence.js mints the proposal record carrying its outcome behind interventionActive, in campaign time only; read once by src/domain/display/newsBody.js :: itemSettlementIds. Admitted ordinary at 40 (d8d7ceee4); 1 read.';
const decisions = template.decisions.map((d) => {
  if (d.subject === 'scanner-transition') {
    return { ...d, decision: 'accept', note: 'the schema-40 -> 41 rung\'s three bookkeeping paths at act 1 (75dd3fc17; the played-worlds writer\'s admission): the live validator to 41, the retired-40 constant, the rung, the new class played-worlds-writer and its one declaration. The corpus definition is unchanged; no unscanned execution input moved.' };
  }
  const row = byId.get(d.rowId);
  if (!row || d.subject !== 'predecessor-reconciliation') refuse(`template row ${d.rowId}`);
  const tagged = row.address.identity === 'outcome on outcome';
  return { ...d, decision: 'accept', note: tagged ? TAGGED : `SAME against the committed schema-40 register: ${row.legacyCount} read(s).` };
});
if (decisions.length !== EXPECTED_SAME + 1) refuse(`decisions ${decisions.length}`);
writeFileSync(outPath, `${JSON.stringify({ ...template, decisions }, null, 2)}\n`, { flag: 'wx' });
console.log(`review written: ${decisions.length} decisions (${EXPECTED_SAME} SAME, 1 transition)`);
```

`dump.mjs`
```js
// STYLE-2-MERGE-8: dump a corpus's settlements as NDJSON (one {key, s} per line) from <tree>'s own generator.
// usage: node dump.mjs <tree> gm|fence <out.ndjson>
import { writeFileSync, appendFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { resolve } from 'node:path';
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
const [tree, which, out] = process.argv.slice(2);
const root = resolve(tree);
const { generateSettlementPipeline } = await import(pathToFileURL(`${root}/src/generators/generateSettlementPipeline.js`).href);
writeFileSync(out, '');
if (which === 'gm') {
  const { goldenCorpus, keyOf } = await import(pathToFileURL(`${root}/tests/helpers/goldenMasterCorpus.js`).href);
  for (const config of goldenCorpus()) {
    const { _seed, ...cfg } = config;
    const s = generateSettlementPipeline(cfg, null, { seed: _seed, customContent: {} });
    appendFileSync(out, JSON.stringify({ key: keyOf(config), s }) + '\n');
  }
} else {
  for (const tier of ['thorp', 'hamlet', 'village', 'town', 'city', 'metropolis'])
    for (const route of ['road', 'isolated', 'port', 'crossroads'])
      for (let i = 0; i < 15; i += 1) {
        const seed = `SUBw4-fence1-${tier}-${route}-${String(i).padStart(3, '0')}`;
        const s = generateSettlementPipeline({ settType: tier, tier, tradeRouteAccess: route, culture: 'germanic' }, null, { seed });
        appendFileSync(out, JSON.stringify({ key: seed, s }) + '\n');
      }
}
console.log('done', which);
```

`fdiff.mjs`
```js
// STYLE-2-MERGE-8: leaf-level field diff of two NDJSON dumps; prints moved rows and, per normalized leaf path
// (array indices -> []), the rows in which that leaf moved. A leaf added or removed counts as moved.
// usage: node fdiff.mjs <a.ndjson> <b.ndjson>
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
const load = (p) => new Map(readFileSync(p, 'utf8').trim().split('\n').map((l) => { const o = JSON.parse(l); return [o.key, o.s]; }));
const A = load(process.argv[2]), B = load(process.argv[3]);
const flat = (v, p, out) => {
  if (v === null || typeof v !== 'object') { out.set(p, JSON.stringify(v)); return out; }
  if (Array.isArray(v)) { if (!v.length) out.set(p, '[]'); v.forEach((x, i) => flat(x, `${p}[${i}]`, out)); return out; }
  const ks = Object.keys(v); if (!ks.length) out.set(p, '{}');
  for (const k of ks) flat(v[k], p ? `${p}.${k}` : k, out); return out;
};
const norm = (p) => p.replace(/\[\d+\]/g, '[]');
const leaf = new Map(); let moved = 0; const byTier = {};
for (const [k, a] of A) {
  const b = B.get(k); if (JSON.stringify(a) === JSON.stringify(b)) continue;
  moved++; const t = k.split(/[|-]/)[k.includes('|') ? 0 : 2]; byTier[t] = (byTier[t] ?? 0) + 1;
  const fa = flat(a, '', new Map()), fb = flat(b, '', new Map()); const hit = new Set();
  for (const p of new Set([...fa.keys(), ...fb.keys()])) if (fa.get(p) !== fb.get(p)) hit.add(norm(p));
  for (const p of hit) leaf.set(p, (leaf.get(p) ?? 0) + 1);
}
console.log(JSON.stringify({ rows: B.size, moved, onlyA: [...A.keys()].filter((k) => !B.has(k)).length, onlyB: [...B.keys()].filter((k) => !A.has(k)).length, byTier }));
for (const [p, n] of [...leaf].sort((x, y) => y[1] - x[1])) console.log(n, p);
```

`probe.py` — the digest-pin probe (run in a detached probe worktree, then `npx vitest run --maxWorkers=1 <the three files>` twice, and once with `git show 12c437de4:src/generators/steps/resolveResources.js` planted; `cmp` the `probe-*` dumps).
```python
# The digest-pin probe as run at point 42 (STYLE-2-MERGE-8), in a DETACHED probe worktree (never the line's checkout):
# it inserts a dump of each pin's live values into the three test files, then the line runs them twice (+ once with the
# pre-EM-B2b2 step planted for editWorldFactResources) and cmp's the dumps; the worktree is discarded afterwards.
def ins(p, anchor, add):
    t=open(p).read(); assert t.count(anchor)==1,(p,anchor); open(p,'w').write(t.replace(anchor, anchor+add))
def top(p):
    t=open(p).read(); i=t.index('\nimport ')+1; open(p,'w').write(t[:i]+"import * as __fs from 'node:fs';\n"+t[i:])
P='/home/user/rec8/probe-'
f='tests/generators/x46NpcBand.test.js'; top(f)
ins(f,"      if (rawBitFormOf(s) !== DARK[seed]) moved.push(seed);\n","      __fs.appendFileSync('"+P+"x46.txt', JSON.stringify([seed, rawBitFormOf(s)]) + '\\n');\n")
f='tests/generators/institutionBudgetPromise.test.js'; top(f)
ins(f,"    const moved = hashes.map((h, i) => (h === BEFORE[i] ? null : i)).filter((i) => i !== null);\n","    __fs.writeFileSync('"+P+"inst.json', JSON.stringify(hashes));\n")
f='tests/domain/editWorldFactResources.test.js'; top(f)
ins(f,"      if (sha(dormant.record) !== PRE_CHANGE[index].derived) drift.push(index);\n","      __fs.appendFileSync('"+P+"ewf.txt', JSON.stringify({ index, derived: sha(dormant.record), fresh: sha(record) }) + '\\n');\n")
ins(f,"      if (sha(replay) !== PRE_CHANGE[index].replay) drift.push(index);\n","      __fs.appendFileSync('"+P+"ewf.txt', JSON.stringify({ index, replay: sha(replay) }) + '\\n');\n")
```

`rekey.py` — the three probe-captured pins written by identity (point 43 on).
```python
# usage (in the line's worktree): python3 rekey.py <probe-dir> <point> <merge-sha> <lane> <record> <cause-clause>
# Re-keys BY IDENTITY the x46 dark digests (fixture), institutionBudgetPromise BEFORE, editWorldFactResources PRE_CHANGE
# from a probe run (probe.py), with a dated note in each file; prints the moved counts. (STYLE-2-MERGE-8)
import json,re,sys
R,PT,M,LANE,REC,CAUSE=sys.argv[1:7]; R=R.rstrip('/')+'/'
def rep(p,o,n):
    t=open(p).read(); assert t.count(o)==1,(p,o[:70]); open(p,'w').write(t.replace(o,n))
fx='tests/fixtures/x46-npc-band-dark-digests.json'; raw=open(fx).read(); old=json.loads(raw)
new=dict(json.loads(l) for l in open(R+'probe-x46.txt')); assert list(new)==list(old)
x46=[k for k in old if old[k]!=new[k]]
open(fx,'w').write(json.dumps(new,indent=2 if '\n  "' in raw else 1)+('\n' if raw.endswith('\n') else ''))
f='tests/generators/institutionBudgetPromise.test.js'; t=open(f).read(); h=json.load(open(R+'probe-inst.json'))
m=re.search(r"const BEFORE = Object\.freeze\(\[\n(.*?)\n\]\);",t,re.S); L=m.group(1).split('\n'); inst=[]
for i,l in enumerate(L):
    v=re.match(r"  '([0-9a-f]{16})', // (\d+)$",l); assert v and int(v.group(2))==i
    if v.group(1)!=h[i]: inst.append(i); L[i]=f"  '{h[i]}', // {i}"
t=t[:m.start(1)]+'\n'.join(L)+t[m.end(1):]
t=t.replace("\nconst BEFORE = Object.freeze([",
f"\n// RE-KEYED BY IDENTITY 2026-11-12 (STYLE-2-MERGE-8, point {PT}): {len(inst)} of 60 moved, none by the budget (densityFactor still\n// forced to 1), by the {LANE} merge {M} ({REC}: {CAUSE}); the line before the merge reads all sixty green.\n// Re-captured at the merge with the factor forced to 1.0, this file's own loop (an untracked probe), twice and equal.\nconst BEFORE = Object.freeze([",1)
open(f,'w').write(t)
```

`homes.mjs` + `hat.sh` — the Wizard News homes at a tree (the contract's CORPUS throw disarmed in a scratch copy) and at a commit, for bisecting a home that left.
```js
// STYLE-2-MERGE-8: the Wizard News homes at <tree>, measured through the contract's own reconstruction with its CORPUS throw
// disarmed in a scratch copy beside the module (removed afterwards). usage: TREE=<tree> node homes.mjs <out.json>
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync, unlinkSync } from 'node:fs';
const R = process.env.TREE + '/';
const src = readFileSync(R + 'scripts/lib/news-voice-contract.mjs', 'utf8');
const armed = "    if (result[key] !== expected) throw new Error(`Wizard News ${key} drifted: expected ${expected}, got ${result[key]}`);";
if (src.split(armed).length !== 2) throw new Error('the throw line moved');
const copy = R + 'scripts/lib/.homes-probe.mjs';
writeFileSync(copy, src.replace(armed, '    void expected;'));
try {
  const { buildObservedCorpus, OBSERVED_SCALAR_FIELDS } = await import(R + 'scripts/lib/observed-shape-corpus.mjs');
  const { reconstructWizardNewsIntroductions } = await import(copy);
  const corpus = await buildObservedCorpus({ scalarFields: OBSERVED_SCALAR_FIELDS });
  const r = reconstructWizardNewsIntroductions(corpus.scalarObservations);
  const homes = [...new Set(r.entries.map(({ fields }) => `${fields.kind}|${fields.impactKind}`))].sort();
  writeFileSync(process.argv[2], JSON.stringify({ ...r, entries: undefined, homeList: homes }, null, 1));
  console.log(r.pulseRoots, r.introductions, r.retirements, r.finalEntries, r.homes);
} finally { unlinkSync(copy); }
```

```sh
#!/bin/sh

s=$1; W=/home/user/stages/hw-$s
cd /home/user/wt-merge8 && git worktree add -q --detach $W $s && ln -s /home/user/wt-merge8/node_modules $W/node_modules
TREE=$W node /home/user/rec8/homes.mjs /home/user/rec8/hb-$s.json > /home/user/rec8/hb-$s.log 2>&1
cd /home/user/wt-merge8 && git worktree remove --force $W
grep -c '"applied|npc_mobilize"' /home/user/rec8/hb-$s.json
```

`pdcounts.mjs` + `rebank.mjs` — the draws behind named pool-dominance rows, and the bounded re-bank of STOP 43a's class (refuses > 2 draws or a row-count change).
```js
// STYLE-2-MERGE-8: draws behind named pool-dominance rows at <tree>, through the guard's own measureDominance.
// usage: node pdcounts.mjs <tree> <pool>... -> JSON {pool: {draws, lines, top1, top5, top1n, top5n}}
const [tree, ...pools] = process.argv.slice(2);
const { measureDominance } = await import(tree + '/scripts/census/pool-dominance.mjs');
const t = measureDominance(); const out = {};
for (const p of pools) { const m = t.get(p); out[p] = m ? { draws: m.draws, lines: m.lines, top1: m.top1, top5: m.top5, top1n: Math.round(m.top1 * m.draws), top5n: Math.round(m.top5 * m.draws) } : null; }
console.log(JSON.stringify(out));
```
```js
// STYLE-2-MERGE-8: re-bank NAMED pool-dominance rows at the merged measure (the chair's ruling at point 43), through the
// guard's own measureDominance and ceil4; refuses a rise of more than two draws, a pool not named, or a row-count change.
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
const [tree, prevJson, ...pools] = process.argv.slice(2);
const G = await import(tree + '/scripts/census/pool-dominance.mjs');
const prev = JSON.parse(readFileSync(prevJson, 'utf8'));
const t = G.measureDominance(); const reg = G.readRegister(); const n0 = Object.keys(reg.pools).length;
for (const p of pools) {
  const m = t.get(p), row = reg.pools[p], b = prev[p];
  if (!m || !row || !b) throw new Error(`refused: ${p} not measured or not registered`);
  for (const k of ['top1', 'top5']) {
    const nb = Math.round(b[k] * b.draws), na = Math.round(m[k] * m.draws);
    if (na - nb > 2) throw new Error(`refused: ${p} ${k} rises ${na - nb} draws`);
    const c = G.ceil4(m[k]);
    if (c > row[k]) { console.log(`${p} ${k} ${row[k]} -> ${c} (${nb}/${b.draws} -> ${na}/${m.draws})`); row[k] = c; }
  }
}
if (Object.keys(reg.pools).length !== n0) throw new Error('refused: row count changed');
writeFileSync(G.REGISTER_PATH, `${JSON.stringify(reg, null, 2)}\n`);
```

## Appendix H. The ninth seat's helpers (in /home/user/rec9/; quoted whole so a tenth seat can recreate them)

Appendix G's helpers were recreated from their quotes with `rec8` → `rec9`, `wt-merge8` → `wt-merge9` and the seat set to 9. New at this seat:

**leafdiff.mjs** (a digest pin's or a dump's moved leaves, by path):

```js
// STYLE-2-MERGE-9: leaf diff of two JSON values; prints count of leaves, moved leaves by normalized path and type, and samples.
// usage: node leafdiff.mjs <a.json> <b.json> [samples]
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
const [a, b, k = '6'] = process.argv.slice(2);
const flat = (v, p, out) => {
  if (v === null || typeof v !== 'object') { out.set(p, v); return out; }
  if (Array.isArray(v)) { if (!v.length) out.set(p, '[]'); v.forEach((x, i) => flat(x, `${p}[${i}]`, out)); return out; }
  const ks = Object.keys(v); if (!ks.length) out.set(p, '{}');
  for (const key of ks) flat(v[key], p ? `${p}.${key}` : key, out); return out;
};
const A = flat(JSON.parse(readFileSync(a, 'utf8')), '', new Map()), B = flat(JSON.parse(readFileSync(b, 'utf8')), '', new Map());
const by = new Map(); const samples = []; let moved = 0, nonString = 0, onlyA = 0, onlyB = 0;
for (const p of new Set([...A.keys(), ...B.keys()])) {
  if (!A.has(p)) { onlyB++; continue; } if (!B.has(p)) { onlyA++; continue; }
  if (A.get(p) === B.get(p)) continue;
  moved++; if (typeof A.get(p) !== 'string' || typeof B.get(p) !== 'string') nonString++;
  const n = p.replace(/\[\d+\]/g, '[]'); by.set(n, (by.get(n) ?? 0) + 1);
  if (samples.length < Number(k)) samples.push([p, A.get(p), B.get(p)]);
}
console.log(JSON.stringify({ leavesA: A.size, leavesB: B.size, moved, nonString, onlyA, onlyB }));
for (const [p, n] of [...by].sort((x, y) => y[1] - x[1])) console.log(n, p);
for (const s of samples) console.log(JSON.stringify(s).slice(0, 400));
```

**witclass.mjs** (the witness's moved leaves classed by the component their old value shows; VISIBLE-BUGS' components — change the patterns per lane):

```js
// STYLE-2-MERGE-9: classify the moved witness leaves by the VISIBLE-BUGS component their OLD value shows (B 'is out to'; C a slash or s's; else other).
// usage (in /home/user/rec9, after witprobe.py at tags 43 and 44): node witclass.mjs
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
const rows = ['__birth_default__', 'quiet_local', 'realistic_regional', 'dramatic_campaign', 'static_campaign', 'narrative_campaign', 'living_realm', 'full_simulation'];
const flat = (v, p, o) => { if (v === null || typeof v !== 'object') { o.set(p, v); return o; } for (const k of Object.keys(v)) flat(v[k], `${p}.${k}`, o); return o; };
const tally = {}; const other = [];
for (const r of rows) {
  const A = flat(JSON.parse(readFileSync(`wit-43-${r}.json`, 'utf8')), '', new Map()), B = flat(JSON.parse(readFileSync(`wit-44-${r}.json`, 'utf8')), '', new Map());
  for (const [p, a] of A) { const b = B.get(p); if (a === b) continue;
    const c = / is out to /.test(a) ? 'B' : /\//.test(a) || /s's\b/.test(a) ? 'C' : 'other';
    tally[c] = (tally[c] ?? 0) + 1; if (c === 'other') other.push([r, p, a, b]); }
}
console.log(JSON.stringify(tally)); for (const o of other) console.log(JSON.stringify(o).slice(0, 500));
```

**tierseed.mjs** (producer 2b's seed re-search by its header's rule (run from the worktree root: `node /path/tierseed.mjs`; fix the absolute import path to your worktree)):

```js
// STYLE-2-MERGE-10: re-search producer 2b's tier-coverage seed by its header's rule.
// For each osr-tier-<i> under CONFIGS[0] (and CONFIGS[3]), run the 12-interval pulse, count tierChange candidates and the first interval they appear.
import { CONFIGS, PULSE_INTERVALS } from '/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-settlement-engine/0b9ba057-dd95-548f-b40e-a0cffc0a7be3/scratchpad/line/scripts/lib/observed-shape-corpus.mjs';
const ROOT = process.cwd();
const { generateSettlementPipeline } = await import(`${ROOT}/src/generators/generateSettlementPipeline.js`);
const { simulateCampaignWorldPulse } = await import(`${ROOT}/src/domain/worldPulse/index.js`);
const { ensureRegionalGraph } = await import(`${ROOT}/src/domain/region/index.js`);
const { buildSpatialDigest } = await import(`${ROOT}/src/domain/spatial/index.js`);
const { makeGridPack, placeSettlements } = await import(`${ROOT}/tests/fixtures/spatialPackFixtures.js`);
const require_ = (await import('node:module')).createRequire(import.meta.url);
const simulationRules = {};
const intervals = PULSE_INTERVALS;
function probe(cfg, seed) {
  const tierSettlement = generateSettlementPipeline(cfg, null, { seed, customContent: {} });
  const pop = tierSettlement?.population ?? tierSettlement?.demographics?.population ?? null;
  const tierId = 'osr-tier';
  const tierPack = makeGridPack({ cols: 10, rows: 8 });
  const [tierPlace] = placeSettlements(tierPack, 1);
  let tierCampaign = {
    id: 'observed-shape-corpus-tier', name: 'x', settlementIds: [tierId],
    worldState: { rngSeed: 'observed-shape-corpus', tick: 1, simulationRules,
      calendar: { elapsedWeeks: 20, year: 1 }, spatialCanonVersion: 1,
      spatialDigest: buildSpatialDigest({ pack: tierPack, placements: [{ id: tierId, cellId: tierPlace.cellId }] }),
      relationshipStates: {}, stressors: [] },
    regionalGraph: ensureRegionalGraph({ edges: [], channels: [] }),
    wizardNews: { currentTick: 1, entries: [] },
  };
  let tierSaves = [{ id: tierId, name: 'Observed tier', phase: 'canon', settlement: tierSettlement,
    campaignState: { phase: 'canon', eventLog: [], locks: {} } }];
  let total = 0, first = null;
  for (let i = 0; i < intervals; i += 1) {
    const out = simulateCampaignWorldPulse({ campaign: tierCampaign, saves: tierSaves, interval: 'one_month', commit: true, now: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z' });
    if (out?.worldState) tierCampaign = { ...tierCampaign, worldState: out.worldState };
    if (out?.wizardNews) tierCampaign = { ...tierCampaign, wizardNews: out.wizardNews };
    if (Array.isArray(out?.saves) && out.saves.length) tierSaves = out.saves;
    const c = (out?.candidates || []).filter((x) => x?.tierChange);
    if (c.length) { total += c.length; if (first === null) first = i; }
  }
  return { pop, total, first };
}
for (const ci of [0, 3]) {
  for (let i = 0; i < 40; i += 1) {
    const seed = `osr-tier-${i}`;
    try { const r = probe(CONFIGS[ci], seed); if (r.total) console.log(`CONFIGS[${ci}] ${seed} pop=${r.pop} candidates=${r.total} firstInterval=${r.first}`); }
    catch (e) { console.log(`CONFIGS[${ci}] ${seed} ERR ${String(e).slice(0,80)}`); }
  }
}
console.log('SEARCH DONE');
```

**wrclass.mjs** (writer reach by kind: growth (violations) and stale, each keyed, KIND-BANKED vs KIND-NEW (fix the absolute paths)):

```js
// STYLE-2-MERGE-10: writer-reach by-kind table for point 47. Growth (violations) and stale, each keyed; a KIND is a `key`.
import { measure, liveViewOf, compareDark, cohortOf } from '/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-settlement-engine/0b9ba057-dd95-548f-b40e-a0cffc0a7be3/scratchpad/line/scripts/check-writer-reach.mjs';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
const frozen = JSON.parse(readFileSync('/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-settlement-engine/0b9ba057-dd95-548f-b40e-a0cffc0a7be3/scratchpad/line/scripts/.writer-reach-baseline.json','utf8'));
const measured = await measure({});
const live = liveViewOf(measured);
const cmp = compareDark(live, frozen);
// map identity -> {key, shape, rows} from live cohort
const liveCohort = new Map(cohortOf(measured.verdicts ?? live.verdicts ?? new Map()).map(r=>[r.identity,r]));
// frozen cohort keys banked at base
const baseKeys = new Set(frozen.darkUnregistered.map(r=>r.key));
const baseIds = new Set(frozen.darkUnregistered.map(r=>r.identity));
const liveById = new Map((live.cohort||[]).map(r=>[r.identity,r]));
function info(id){ const r = liveById.get(id) || liveCohort.get(id); return r||{key:'?',shape:'?',rows:'?'}; }
console.log('VIOLATIONS (growth):', cmp.violations.length);
const vb={}; for(const id of cmp.violations){ const r=info(id); const k=r.key; (vb[k]=vb[k]||[]).push([id, r.shape, r.rows, baseKeys.has(k)?'KIND-BANKED':'KIND-NEW']); }
for(const k of Object.keys(vb).sort()){ console.log(`  key=${k} n=${vb[k].length} ${vb[k][0][3]}`); for(const row of vb[k]) console.log('     ', JSON.stringify(row)); }
console.log('STALE:', cmp.stale.length);
const sb={}; for(const row of cmp.stale){ const k=(frozen.darkUnregistered.find(x=>x.identity===row.identity)||{}).key||'?'; (sb[k]=sb[k]||0); sb[k]++; }
for(const k of Object.keys(sb).sort()) console.log(`  key=${k} n=${sb[k]}`);
console.log('STRUCK:', cmp.struck.length);
console.log('SENTINEL:', (cmp.sentinel||[]).length, JSON.stringify((cmp.sentinel||[]).slice(0,3)));
```

**rung42.py** (rung 41 -> 42, the migration tool's rows (its `edit()` asserts each anchor occurs exactly once); run in the worktree root):

```python
# STYLE-2-MERGE-10: rung 41 -> 42 rows (the chair's ruling on STOP 47a, condition 2: a row, not a branch).
import sys
def edit(path, pairs):
    s = open(path).read()
    for old, new in pairs:
        n = s.count(old)
        if n != 1: sys.exit(f"ANCHOR x{n} in {path}: {old[:70]!r}")
        s = s.replace(old, new)
    open(path, 'w').write(s)
M = 'scripts/migrate-observed-shape-readers.mjs'
RAT = """/* ⭐⭐ THE SCHEMA 41 → 42 RUNG — THE AGREEMENT LANE'S TIER SEED (STYLE-2-MERGE point 47, COHERENCE-AGREE; the
 *  chair's ruling on STOP 47a, 2026-11-13 10:20 UTC: "(B), MIGRATE NOW", one package, a row and not a branch).
 *
 *  THE CAUSE: the agreement lane's generation moved the tier seed's town out of the overlap. Producer 2b's
 *  `osr-tier-14` under CONFIGS[0] no longer lands in the town/city overlap, so its pulse minted no tier candidate
 *  in twelve intervals and the corpus builder THREW (observedShapeReaders, writerReach and proseFamilyContract
 *  failed at load). Re-searched by producer 2b's own rule over `osr-tier-0..39` under CONFIGS[0]: the minters are
 *  osr-tier-19 (pop 2477, interval 5), -25 (2394) and -28 (2282); the lowest, osr-tier-19, is taken. The corpus
 *  builder is a DETECTOR SOURCE, so the seed is re-governed here: schema 41's topology, tag law, roster and bank
 *  UNCHANGED; the reconciliation (every moved row classed WITNESSED / BINDING LOST, read by the chair before the
 *  re-freeze) is recorded in the re-freeze commit. The delta is FOUR paths — the corpus builder and the three
 *  bookkeeping paths; see `AGREE_TIER_SEED_SCANNER_DELTA_PATHS`. The commit pair holds as at every rung: this
 *  rung lands walker-red, and its re-freeze lands next. */
export const AGREE_TIER_SEED_TARGET_SCHEMA = 42;
"""
edit(M, [
  ("  validateSchema40Baseline,\n} from './lib/observed-shape-baseline.mjs';",
   "  validateSchema40Baseline,\n  validateSchema41Baseline,\n} from './lib/observed-shape-baseline.mjs';"),
  (" *   schema 40 -> 41 (LIVE)   THE PLAYED-WORLDS WRITER'S ADMISSION",
   " *   schema 41 -> 42 (LIVE)   THE AGREEMENT LANE'S TIER SEED (STYLE-2-MERGE point 47, the chair's ruling on STOP\n"
   " *     47a): one rung, one cause — producer 2b's tier seed re-searched by its own rule (osr-tier-14 -> osr-tier-19)\n"
   " *     after COHERENCE-AGREE moved osr-tier-14's town out of the overlap; topology, tag law, roster and bank\n"
   " *     (78/52) unchanged. Its full rationale lives beside `AGREE_TIER_SEED_TARGET_SCHEMA`.\n"
   " *\n"
   " *   schema 40 -> 41 (RETIRED) THE PLAYED-WORLDS WRITER'S ADMISSION"),
  ("export const PLAYED_WORLDS_TARGET_SCHEMA = 41;\n",
   "export const PLAYED_WORLDS_TARGET_SCHEMA = 41;\n\n" + RAT),
  ("  [PLAYED_WORLDS_TARGET_SCHEMA]: Object.freeze({ bankedReads: 78, taggedRows: 52 }),\n",
   "  [PLAYED_WORLDS_TARGET_SCHEMA]: Object.freeze({ bankedReads: 78, taggedRows: 52 }),\n"
   "  [AGREE_TIER_SEED_TARGET_SCHEMA]: Object.freeze({ bankedReads: 78, taggedRows: 52 }),\n"),
  ("export const PLAYED_WORLDS_SCANNER_DELTA_PATHS = Object.freeze([\n  'scripts/check-observed-shape-readers.mjs',\n  'scripts/lib/observed-shape-baseline.mjs',\n  'scripts/migrate-observed-shape-readers.mjs',\n]);\n",
   "export const PLAYED_WORLDS_SCANNER_DELTA_PATHS = Object.freeze([\n  'scripts/check-observed-shape-readers.mjs',\n  'scripts/lib/observed-shape-baseline.mjs',\n  'scripts/migrate-observed-shape-readers.mjs',\n]);\n\n"
   "/** The schema-41 → 42 delta: FOUR paths — the corpus builder (producer 2b's tier seed) and the three bookkeeping paths. */\n"
   "export const AGREE_TIER_SEED_SCANNER_DELTA_PATHS = Object.freeze([\n  'scripts/check-observed-shape-readers.mjs',\n  'scripts/lib/observed-shape-baseline.mjs',\n  'scripts/lib/observed-shape-corpus.mjs',\n  'scripts/migrate-observed-shape-readers.mjs',\n]);\n"),
  ("const PLAYED_WORLDS_SCANNER_TRANSITION_POLICY =\n  'schema-40-to-41-played-worlds-writer-v1';\n",
   "const PLAYED_WORLDS_SCANNER_TRANSITION_POLICY =\n  'schema-40-to-41-played-worlds-writer-v1';\nconst AGREE_TIER_SEED_SCANNER_TRANSITION_POLICY =\n  'schema-41-to-42-tier-seed-v1';\n"),
  ("  [PLAYED_WORLDS_TARGET_SCHEMA]: PUBLIC_PROSE_TARGET_SCHEMA,\n",
   "  [PLAYED_WORLDS_TARGET_SCHEMA]: PUBLIC_PROSE_TARGET_SCHEMA,\n  [AGREE_TIER_SEED_TARGET_SCHEMA]: PLAYED_WORLDS_TARGET_SCHEMA,\n"),
  ("  [PUBLIC_PROSE_TARGET_SCHEMA]: validateSchema40Baseline,\n",
   "  [PUBLIC_PROSE_TARGET_SCHEMA]: validateSchema40Baseline,\n  [PLAYED_WORLDS_TARGET_SCHEMA]: validateSchema41Baseline,\n"),
  ("  [PLAYED_WORLDS_TARGET_SCHEMA, Object.freeze({\n    deltaPaths: PLAYED_WORLDS_SCANNER_DELTA_PATHS,",
   "  [AGREE_TIER_SEED_TARGET_SCHEMA, Object.freeze({\n    deltaPaths: AGREE_TIER_SEED_SCANNER_DELTA_PATHS,\n    inputPaths: BANKED_EXPLAINED_WRITER_SCANNER_INPUT_PATHS,\n    policy: AGREE_TIER_SEED_SCANNER_TRANSITION_POLICY,\n    reviewableUnscannedMovement: true,\n  })],\n"
   "  [PLAYED_WORLDS_TARGET_SCHEMA, Object.freeze({\n    deltaPaths: PLAYED_WORLDS_SCANNER_DELTA_PATHS,"),
  (": (currentPath ? RETIRED_EXACT_TARGET_SCHEMA : PLAYED_WORLDS_TARGET_SCHEMA);",
   ": (currentPath ? RETIRED_EXACT_TARGET_SCHEMA : AGREE_TIER_SEED_TARGET_SCHEMA);"),
  ("    TRAIN_CAR4_TARGET_SCHEMA, PUBLIC_PROSE_TARGET_SCHEMA, PLAYED_WORLDS_TARGET_SCHEMA].includes(targetSchema)) {\n    throw new Error(`observed-shape --target-schema must be ${PLAYED_WORLDS_TARGET_SCHEMA} (live played-worlds leaf),`\n",
   "    TRAIN_CAR4_TARGET_SCHEMA, PUBLIC_PROSE_TARGET_SCHEMA, PLAYED_WORLDS_TARGET_SCHEMA,\n    AGREE_TIER_SEED_TARGET_SCHEMA].includes(targetSchema)) {\n    throw new Error(`observed-shape --target-schema must be ${AGREE_TIER_SEED_TARGET_SCHEMA} (live tier-seed leaf),`\n      + ` ${PLAYED_WORLDS_TARGET_SCHEMA} (retired played-worlds leaf),`\n"),
])
print("migrate edited")
```

```python
   "    expect(declaredBankOf(AGREE_TIER_SEED_TARGET_SCHEMA)).toEqual({ bankedReads: 78, taggedRows: 52 });\n    expect(declaredBankOf(BASELINE_SCHEMA)).toEqual(declaredBankOf(AGREE_TIER_SEED_TARGET_SCHEMA));"),
  ("    expect(validateSchema41Baseline(live)).toBe(live); // the played-worlds rung (41), 2026-11-11 (STYLE-2-MERGE closing act 5)\n",
   "    expect(validateSchema42Baseline(live)).toBe(live); // the tier-seed rung (42), 2026-11-13 (STYLE-2-MERGE point 47, STOP 47a)\n    expect(() => validateSchema41Baseline(live)).toThrow(/is not schema 41/);\n"),
])
S='tests/lint/observedShapeSentinel.test.js'; s=open(S).read()
old="    expect(BASELINE_SCHEMA).toBe(41); // the played-worlds rung, 2026-11-11 (STYLE-2-MERGE closing act 5)"
assert s.count(old)==2
s=s.replace(old,"    expect(BASELINE_SCHEMA).toBe(42); // the tier-seed rung, 2026-11-13 (STYLE-2-MERGE point 47, STOP 47a)"); open(S,'w').write(s)
print('tests edited')
```
